\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)_{\downarrow}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}(\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}))_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\,,

the second step follows from triangle inequality, and the third step follows from
Lemma

1.8
and
Corollary

B.2
.

This implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right),\quad\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,.

For the furthermore part, let the eigendecomposition of
\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}
be as follows:

\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}=\boldsymbol{U}^{\dagger}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{U}_{\downarrow}.

We define an invertible matrix
\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}
as:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}:=\boldsymbol{U}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{-1/2}_{\downarrow}\,.

(E.20)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{Q_{\downarrow}}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}=\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\,,

Using Eq. (
E.18
), we obtain that
\|\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{-1}_{\downarrow}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)
. This implies that
\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}\in\mathbb{C}^{r\times r}
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{Q_{\downarrow}}\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right),\quad\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{Q_{\downarrow}}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

where the first equation follows from
\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{Q_{\downarrow}}\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}=\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{-1}
, and the second equation follows from
\boldsymbol{P}_{Q_{\downarrow}}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{Q_{\downarrow}}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}=\boldsymbol{U}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{-1}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r})\boldsymbol{U}_{\downarrow}
.

The claim is then proved.
∎

Claim E.4
(Neumann series truncation).

It holds that

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(-\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right)^{k}\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\underbrace{\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}}_{\text{zeroth order term}}-\underbrace{\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)}_{\text{first order term}}+\underbrace{\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{2}}_{\text{second order term}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{3}\log^{3}n}{\mu^{3}_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,.

Proof.

For the Neumann series term in Eq. (
E.10
):

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(-\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right)^{k}\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\,,

(E.21)

we first bound the middle bracket:

\displaystyle\left\|(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\left(2\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}\right\|_{2}\left(2\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{z}n}}\right)\,,

(E.22)

where the first step follows from triangle inequality, the second step is straightforward, the third step follows from the definition of
\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}
and Eq. (
5.4
).

The first-order term (i.e.,
k=1
) in Eq. (
E.21
) can be approximated as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}+\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}-(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right\|_{2}

(E.23)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right)\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\left(\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{z}n}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from triangle inequality, the second step follows from Eq. (
E.18
) and Eq. (
E.22
), and the last step is by direct calculation.

By a similar calculation, we can show that the second-order term (i.e.,
k=2
) in Eq. (
E.21
) can be approximated by

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right)^{2}\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}-\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}-(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{2}\right\|_{2}

(E.24)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{z}n}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}^{2}n^{2}}\right)\,.

Indeed, this term can be further simplified:

\displaystyle\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}-(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{2}-\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{2}\right\|_{2}

(E.25)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{z}n}}\right)^{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{3}\log^{3}n}{\mu^{3}_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first inequality follows from Eq. (
5.4
) for
\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\|_{2}
and Eq. (
E.18
) for
\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\|_{2}
.

The higher-order terms (i.e.,
k>2
) in Eq. (
E.21
) can be directly bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\sum^{\infty}_{k=3}\left(-\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right)^{k}\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}

(E.26)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=3}^{\infty}\left\|(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{k}\left\|(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}^{k}\cdot\left\|(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=3}^{\infty}{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{z}n}}\right)^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{3}\log^{3}n}{\mu^{3}_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from triangle inequality, the second step follows from Eq. (
E.18
) and Eq. (
E.22
), and the last step follows from the geometric summation.

Combining Eq. (
E.23
) to Eq. (
E.26
) together, we get that

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(-\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)\right)^{k}\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{-1}-\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}+(\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)+\left((\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right)^{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{3}\log^{3}n}{\mu^{3}_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,.

The claim is then proved.
∎

E.3
Proofs for Step 3: Error cancellation in the Taylor expansion

E.3.1
Establishing the first equation in
Eq.

5.10

See
5.5

Proof.

In this proof, we often use the following observations:

\boldsymbol{P}_{v}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}=\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,,

(E.27a)

\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-k}_{r}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}=\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-k}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}n}\right)^{k+1}\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}}\quad\forall k\geq 0\,.

(E.27b)

They are proved in
Claim

E.5
.

Plugging the formula of
\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}
into the first equation of Eq. (
5.10
), we obtain
Eq.

5.13a
:

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\cdot\Big\|(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger})^{-1}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\left(\boldsymbol{P}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right)\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\left(\boldsymbol{P}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right)(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\right)+{\cal O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{P}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}\left(1+\big\|(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\big\|_{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}}\right)\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}}\right)\cdot{\cal O}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1})_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1})_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}+\left\|(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2})_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2})_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1})_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1})_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right\|_{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\Big\|\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\Big\|_{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from multiplying
\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger})^{-1}
on the right and pulling out
\|(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger})^{-1}\|_{2}
, the second step follows from
\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{-1}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
by Eq. (
1.14
), the third step follows from triangle inequality, the fourth step follows from
\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{-1}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
again and
\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\|_{2},\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\|_{2}\leq\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}\|_{2}
, the fifth step follows from Eq. (
5.4
), Eq. (
5.3
), and
\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\|_{2}=1
, the sixth step follows by direct calculation, the seventh step follows from
\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}=\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}_{1}^{Q}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}_{2}^{Q}
by Eq. (
5.12
), where
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1},\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}
are defined in Eq. (
5.11
), the eighth step follows from
\|\boldsymbol{P}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
,
\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
, and
\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\|_{2}=1
, and the last step uses the bound for
\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\|_{2}
derived from its definition Eq. (
5.11
).

We first consider
\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\uparrow}
. Notice that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}=

\displaystyle~\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle+\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v},

where the first step follows from the definition of
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}
.
To bound the second term, for any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{E}_{1}\|_{2}^{k}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\|_{2}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{k+1}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~{\cal O}(1)\cdot(n\mu_{\rm tail})^{k}\cdot{\cal O}(\alpha\sqrt{n\log n})\cdot 1\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\cdot{\cal O}(n\mu_{r})^{-(k+1)}\cdot{\cal O}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{\cal O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)\cdot\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}

where the third step uses
Lemma

1.8
for the first term, Eq. (
C.1
) for the second term,
Lemma

C.1
for the third term, Eq. (
1.14
) for the fifth and seventh terms, Eq. (
B.2
) for the sixth term. Thus,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\leq

\displaystyle~\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}{\cal O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)\cdot\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle+{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}

\displaystyle+\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,.

To bound the second term in the above equation, for any
k\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~{\cal O}(1)\cdot\|\boldsymbol{E}_{1}\|_{2}^{k}\cdot\|\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\right)^{k+1}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~(n\mu_{\rm tail})^{k}\cdot{\cal O}(\alpha\sqrt{n\log n})\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}n}\right)^{k+2}\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~{\cal O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)^{k}\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}^{2}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}

where the first step uses
Lemma

1.8
to bound
\|\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\|_{2}
and
\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\|_{2}
, the second step uses Eq. (
C.1
),
Lemma

C.1
, Eq. (
E.27b
) to bound the three terms, respectively. Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\leq

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}

(E.28)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}{\cal O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)^{k}\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}^{2}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}^{2}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,.

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

(E.29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}\cdot\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\cdot(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}+\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}\cdot\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\cdot(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}+{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}}\right)\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})+\left\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right)_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})

\displaystyle+\left\|\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}^{2}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right)_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from inserting
\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}
in the middle, the second step follows from Eq. (
E.8
) for
\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\|_{2}
and Eq. (
1.14
) for
\|\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\|_{2}
, the third step follows from re-grouping terms, the fourth step follows from Eq. (
E.27a
), the fifth step follows from Eq. (
E.8
), the sixth step follows from Eq. (
E.28
), and the last step follows from
\|\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\|_{2}=1
.

We also notice the following bound to get rid of some terms involving
\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}
and
\boldsymbol{E}^{k}_{1}
for
k\geq 1
:

\displaystyle\left\|\sum^{\infty}_{k=1}\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right\|_{2}

(E.30)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right\|_{2}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{1}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{E}_{1}\right\|_{2}^{k-1}\left\|\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\right\|_{2}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right\|_{2}\left\|\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-1}\right\|_{2}^{k+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=1}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}}\right)\cdot(n\mu_{\rm tail})^{k-1}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha\sqrt{n\log n}\right)\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(n\mu_{r})^{k+1}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)^{k}\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}\cdot\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}^{2}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where in the third step, we use
Corollary

B.2
to bound
\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right\|_{2}
, use
Claim

E.6
to bound
\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{1}\right\|_{2}
, use Eq. (
C.1
) to bound
\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{k-1}_{1}\right\|_{2}
, use
Lemma

C.1
to bound
\|\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\|_{2}
, and use the fact that
\|\diag(\mu_{\rm dom})^{-1}\|_{2}=\mu_{r}^{-1}
.

Plugging Eq. (
E.30
) into Eq. (
E.28
) and Eq. (
E.29
), we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}=

\displaystyle~\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

and

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}\right)_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-1}\right)_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the
\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}
terms come from the
k=0
case in Eq. (
E.28
) and Eq. (
E.29
).

The proof of the lemma is completed.
∎

See
5.6

Proof.

We analyze Eq. (
5.13b
) and Eq. (
5.13c
) column-by-column.

For convenience, we define
\lambda_{i}\in\mathbb{C}
and
\boldsymbol{F}_{i}\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times n}
for each
1\leq i\leq r
as follows:

\displaystyle\lambda_{i}:=

\displaystyle~n\mu_{i},

(E.31)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{F}_{i}:=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{\lambda_{i}}\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{i}}\right)^{k}-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\,.

(E.32)

Observe that

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\begin{bmatrix}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})&\cdots&\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})\end{bmatrix}\cdot\diag(\boldsymbol{\lambda}^{-(k+1)})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left[\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}^{k+1}}\quad\cdots\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\lambda_{r}^{k+1}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\cdots\quad\frac{1}{\lambda_{r}}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{r}}\right)^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right]\,.

where

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{i}):=\begin{bmatrix}1&z_{i}&z_{i}^{2}&\cdots&z_{i}^{n-1}\end{bmatrix}^{\top}\in\mathbb{C}^{n}\,.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left[\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\cdots\quad\frac{1}{\lambda_{r}}\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right]\,.

Combining the above two equations together and summing over
k
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\boldsymbol{E}_{1}^{k}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k+1)}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{2})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\cdots\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{r}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right]\,.

By Eq. (
5.13b
) and Eq. (
5.13c
), we have

\displaystyle\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

(E.33)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{2})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\cdots\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{r}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right]_{\uparrow}

\displaystyle-\left[\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{2})}{\sqrt{n}}\quad\cdots\quad\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{r}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right]_{\downarrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

Without loss of generality, we only consider the first column of Eq. (
E.33
):

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\downarrow}z_{1}^{-1}

(E.34)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{k}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=0}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{k}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}z_{i}^{-1}\right)_{\downarrow}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\uparrow}+\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}z_{1}^{-1}\right)_{\downarrow}\,.

Recall

\boldsymbol{E}_{2}=\begin{bmatrix}E_{0}&\overline{E_{1}}&\overline{E_{2}}&\cdots&\overline{E_{n-1}}\\
E_{1}&E_{0}&\overline{E_{1}}&\cdots&\overline{E_{n-2}}\\
E_{2}&E_{1}&E_{0}&\cdots&\overline{E_{n-3}}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
E_{n-1}&E_{n-2}&E_{n-3}&\cdots&E_{0}\end{bmatrix}.

Define vectors

\boldsymbol{p}_{0}:=\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}},\quad\boldsymbol{q}_{0}:=\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}z^{-1}_{1}\,.

Because
\lambda_{1}=n\mu_{1}\geq n\mu_{r}
and
\|\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})\|_{\infty}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\|_{\infty}\leq\frac{\|\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\|_{1}}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}\leq\frac{n\cdot{\cal O}(\alpha\sqrt{\log n})}{\sqrt{n}\cdot\mu_{r}n}={\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}}\right)\,,

(E.35)

where the second step follows from
|E_{i}|={\cal O}(\alpha\sqrt{\log n})
. We also have

\|\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\|_{\infty}=\mathcal{O}(\alpha\sqrt{\log n}/(\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}))
since
|z_{1}|=1
. Using the structure of
\boldsymbol{E}_{2}
, we obtain

\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}=\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}\begin{bmatrix}-E_{1}z^{-1}_{1}+\overline{E_{n-1}}z^{n-1}_{1}\\
-E_{2}z^{-1}_{1}+\overline{E_{n-2}}z^{n-1}_{1}\\
\vdots\\
-E_{n-1}z^{-1}_{1}+\overline{E_{1}}z^{n-1}_{1}\\
\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{\infty}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

(E.36)

where we use
|E_{i}|=\mathcal{O}(\alpha\sqrt{\log n})
and
\lambda_{1}\geq\mu_{r}n
in the last equality.

We first consider the terms in Eq. (
E.34
) that involves the second term of
\boldsymbol{F}_{1}
:

\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\right)_{\downarrow}\,.

Note that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)_{i,j}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{r}z_{l}^{i-j}\quad\forall~1\leq i,j\leq n\,,

which implies that
\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}
is also a Toeplize matrix (since the
(i,j)
-th entry only depends on
i-j
), and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right\|_{\text{entry},\infty}=\frac{r}{n}\,,

(E.37)

where
\left\|A\right\|_{\text{entry},\infty}=\max_{i,j}|A_{i,j}|
.
Thus, for any
1\leq i\leq n-1
,

\displaystyle\left(\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\Big)_{\uparrow}-\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\Big)_{\downarrow}\right)_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\Big)_{i,j}(\boldsymbol{p}_{0})_{j}-\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\Big)_{i+1,j}(\boldsymbol{q}_{0})_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\sum_{j=2}^{n-1}\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\Big)_{i,j}\left((\boldsymbol{p}_{0})_{\uparrow}-(\boldsymbol{q}_{0})_{\downarrow}\right)_{j}+\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\Big)_{i,1}((\boldsymbol{p}_{0})_{\uparrow})_{1}

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\Big)_{i+1,n}((\boldsymbol{q}_{0})_{\downarrow})_{n-1}\,,

where the second step follows from the Toeplize structure of the matrix. Therefore, by triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}

(E.38)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right\|_{\text{entry},\infty}\cdot\left((n-2)\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\right\|_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{r}{n}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(n\cdot\frac{\alpha}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}+\frac{\alpha}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r\alpha}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}\right)\,.

where the second step follows from Eq. (
E.35
)-Eq. (
E.37
).

Now, we consider the first term of
\boldsymbol{F}_{1}
. Define

\boldsymbol{p}_{k}:=\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)\boldsymbol{p}_{k-1},\quad\boldsymbol{q}_{k}:=\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)\boldsymbol{q}_{k-1}

for
k\geq 1
. We first notice

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right\|_{\text{entry},\infty}=\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\max_{i,j}\sum_{l=r+1}^{d}|\mu_{l}z_{l}^{i-j}|\leq\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\lambda_{1}}={\cal O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}n}\right)\,,

(E.39)

where the second step follows from the definition of
\mu_{\rm tail}
.
Then, we have

\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k+1}\right\|_{\infty}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right\|_{\infty},\quad\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{k+1}\right\|_{\infty}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right\|_{\infty}\,.

Furthermore, because
\boldsymbol{E}_{1}
is also a Toeplize matrix, similar to Eq. (
E.38
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k+1}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k+1}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{E}_{1}/\lambda_{1}\|_{\text{entry},\infty}\cdot\left((n-2)\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right\|_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}n}(\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right\|_{\infty})\right)\,,

where the second step follows from Eq. (
E.39
).
The above two equations imply that

\displaystyle n\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k+1}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k+1}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k+1}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{k+1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)\cdot\left(n\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+(\left\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right\|_{\infty}+\left\|\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right\|_{\infty})\right)\,.

Plugging in the values of
k=0
in Eq. (
E.35
) and Eq. (
E.36
), we obtain for any
k>0
,

\displaystyle n\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+\|\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\|_{\infty}+\|\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\|_{\infty}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)^{k}\cdot\frac{\alpha}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}}\,,

which implies that

\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{p}_{k}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\boldsymbol{q}_{k}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}}\right)^{k}\cdot\frac{\alpha}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}~~~\forall k\geq 0\,.

(E.40)

This gives a bound for each term in the summation of the first term of
\boldsymbol{F}_{i}
.

Therefore, we get that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\downarrow}z^{-1}_{1}\right\|_{2}

(E.41)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left\|\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\downarrow}z^{-1}_{1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left\|(\boldsymbol{p}_{k})_{\uparrow}-(\boldsymbol{q}_{k})_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}+\sqrt{n}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{p}_{0}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{q}_{0}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha}{\mu_{r}n}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r\alpha}{\mu_{r}n}\right)=~\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r\alpha}{\mu_{r}n}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from
\|x\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{n}\|x\|_{\infty}
for any
x\in\mathbb{C}^{n}
, the second step follows from Eq. (
E.34
), and the third step follows from Eq. (
E.38
) and Eq. (
E.40
).

Performing the same computation as Eq. (
E.34
) to Eq. (
E.41
) for every
1\leq i\leq r
, we derive

\max_{1\leq i\leq r}\left\|\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{i}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{i})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{i}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{i}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{i})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{i}}\right)_{\downarrow}z^{-1}_{i}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r\alpha}{\mu_{r}n}\right)\,.

It implies that

\displaystyle\Big\|\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\left[\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{1}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\downarrow}z_{1}^{-1}\quad\cdots\quad\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{r}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{r}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{r})}{\sqrt{n}}\right)_{\downarrow}z_{r}^{-1}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\sqrt{r}\max_{i}\left\|\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{i}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{i})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{i}}\right)_{\uparrow}-\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{F}_{i}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{i})}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{i}}\right)_{\downarrow}z^{-1}_{i}\right\|_{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{r^{1.5}\alpha}{\mu_{r}n}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from Eq. (
E.33
), the second step follows from
\|A\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{r}\max_{i\in[n]}\|A_{i}\|_{2}
for any
A\in\mathbb{C}^{n\times r}
, the last step follows from Eq. (
E.41
).

The lemma is then proved.
∎

E.3.2
Establishing the second equation in
Eq.

5.10

See
5.8

Proof.

For simplicity, we mainly consider the first term
\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}
. Another term
\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}
can be bounded similarly.

First, we notice that

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{\uparrow}^{Q}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\downarrow}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}+\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}+{\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\diag^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from
\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v}
by
Lemma

1.8
, the second step follows from
\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})_{\downarrow}=\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})_{\downarrow}\cdot\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})
by the structure of the Vandermonde matrix, the third step follows from
\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
by Eq. (
1.14
),
\|\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\|_{2}=1
by definition, and
\big\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\big\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
by
Corollary

B.2
,
the fourth step follows from Eq. (
1.14
) and Eq. (
5.4
), and the last step follows from
Lemma

1.8
again.

Thus, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{\uparrow}^{Q}\right\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\diag^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)

(E.42)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the second step follows from
\|\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\diag^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
. Moreover,
\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}
can be further bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}^{\dagger}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{E}_{\uparrow}^{Q}\right\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}

(E.43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}+{\cal O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{E}_{\uparrow}^{Q}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{-1}\right\|_{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{P}^{-1}\right\|_{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)_{\uparrow}^{\dagger}\left(\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}+\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from Eq. (
5.12
), the second step follows from
Lemma

1.8
, the third step follows from triangle inequality and
\big\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\big\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
by
Corollary

B.2
, the fourth step follows from
Eqs.

1.14
and
5.4
, the fifth step follows from the sub-multiplicitivity of the spectral norm, the sixth step follows from
\left\|\boldsymbol{P}^{-1}\right\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
by Eq. (
5.3
).

Let
\boldsymbol{V}_{r}:=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})
. Then, by
Eqs.

E.42
and
E.43
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}_{\uparrow}^{Q}\right\|_{2}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,.

(E.44)

For the second term
\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}
in the second equation of Eq. (
5.10
), we notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\left\|(\boldsymbol{P}_{v})^{-1}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\right)\,,

where we use
\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{-1}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
by Eq. (
1.14
) in the last step. Similar to Eq. (
E.43
), we can show that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{E}^{Q}_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\downarrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\downarrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\downarrow}\right\|_{2}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}(\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n)^{1.5}}\right)\,.

(E.45)

Combining Eq. (
E.44
) and Eq. (
E.45
) together, the lemma is proved.
∎

See
5.9

Proof.

First, we consider
\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}
. Recall from Eq. (
5.11
) that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}={\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\boldsymbol{\Pi}_{\boldsymbol{Q}^{\perp}_{r}}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}_{2}=:{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\widetilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}_{2}^{\perp}

(E.46)

Since the column space of
\boldsymbol{V}_{r}
equals to the column space of
\boldsymbol{Q}_{r}
, we note that
\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\right)=\boldsymbol{0}_{r}
, where
\boldsymbol{0}_{r}
is a
r
by
r
matrix with all zero entries. Although
\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}
is the multiplication with
\uparrow
, the remaining term for
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}
is small. In particular, for any
(i,j)\in[r]\times[r]
, we have the following

\displaystyle\left|\left(\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)_{i,j}\right|

(E.47)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)\right)_{i,j}-(\overline{\boldsymbol{V}_{r}})_{n,i}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P})_{n,j}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|(\overline{\boldsymbol{V}_{r}})_{n,i}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P})_{n,j}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\cdot\frac{|\overline{z^{n-1}_{i}}|}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\left|\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}_{2}^{\perp}\right)_{n,j}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

where the first step follows from the definition of
(\cdot)_{\uparrow}
, the second step follows from
(\boldsymbol{V}_{r})^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)=\boldsymbol{0}_{r}\cdot\boldsymbol{P}=\boldsymbol{0}_{r}
, the third step follows from Eq. (
E.46
), and the fourth step follows from
\big|\big(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}_{2}^{\perp}\big)_{n,j}\big|\leq\big\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}_{2}^{\perp}\big\|_{\text{entry},\infty}\leq\big\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{Q}}_{2}^{\perp}\big\|_{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
. It implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{2}\boldsymbol{P}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{F}\leq r\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}\log n}{\mu^{2}_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,.

(E.48)

Next, we deal with
\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}
. First, because
\boldsymbol{P}_{v}
is almost unitary according to
Lemma

1.8
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\right\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\left\|(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger})^{-1}\right\|_{2}

(E.49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}\right)\,.

Without loss of generality, we bound the norm of the first column of
\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}
.
By using Eq. (
5.13b
) to approximate
\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)[:,1]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{i}}\right)^{k}\right)_{\uparrow}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|_{2}

(E.50)

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}\right)_{\uparrow}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|_{2}

(E.51)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}^{2}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where
\lambda_{1}=\mu_{1}n\geq\mu_{r}n
.

Then, we bound Eq. (
E.50
) and Eq. (
E.51
) separately.

For Eq. (
E.50
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\sum^{\infty}_{k=1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)^{k}\right)_{\uparrow}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|_{2}

(E.52)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left(\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\left\|\boldsymbol{E}_{1}\right\|_{2}^{k}}{|\lambda_{1}|^{k}}\right)\cdot\frac{\|\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})\|_{2}}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\cal O}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{n\mu_{\rm tail}}{\mu_{r}n}\right)^{k}\cdot\frac{\alpha\sqrt{n\log n}\cdot\sqrt{n}}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{1}}{\lambda_{1}}\right)_{\uparrow}\right\|_{2}\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\mu_{\rm tail}}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}\lambda_{1}}\right)\cdot{\cal O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the first step follows from the triangle inequality, the second step follows from Eq. (
C.1
),
Lemma

C.1
, the definition of
\lambda_{1}
, and
\|\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})\|_{2}=\sqrt{n}
, the third step follows from
\mu_{\rm tail}<\mu_{r}
, the fourth step follows from
Claim

E.6
, and the last step follows from
\lambda_{1}\geq\mu_{r}n
and
\mu_{\rm tail}<\mu_{r}
.

For Eq. (
E.51
), we notice that

\displaystyle\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}\right)_{\uparrow}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\quad\boldsymbol{0}_{1}\right]-\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left[\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\quad\boldsymbol{0}_{1}\right]-\frac{n-1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}+\frac{n-1}{n}\left\|\boldsymbol{I}_{r}-\frac{1}{n-1}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\quad\boldsymbol{0}_{1}\right]-\frac{n-1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}+{\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left[\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\quad\boldsymbol{0}_{1}\right]-\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}+{\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left[\boldsymbol{0}_{r\times n-1}\quad\begin{matrix}z_{1}^{1-n}\\
\vdots\\
z_{r}^{1-n}\end{matrix}\right]+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,,

where
\boldsymbol{0}_{1}\in\mathbb{C}^{r}
is an all-zero vector, the second step follows from
(\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}))_{\uparrow}=\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})
by definition, the third step follows from
Corollary

B.2
, the fourth step follows from
\|\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\|_{2}={\cal O}(1)
, and the last step is by direct calculation.
Plugging it into Eq. (
E.51
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left(\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\right)_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\frac{1}{\lambda_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}-\boldsymbol{V}_{r}\boldsymbol{V}_{r}^{\dagger}\right)_{\uparrow}\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\sqrt{n}}\right\|_{2}

(E.53)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left[\boldsymbol{0}_{r\times n-1}\quad\begin{matrix}z_{1}^{1-n}\\
\vdots\\
z_{r}^{1-n}\end{matrix}\right]\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\frac{\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})}{\lambda_{1}\sqrt{n}}\right\|_{2}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{bmatrix}z_{1}^{1-n}\\
\vdots\\
z_{r}^{1-n}\end{bmatrix}\right\|_{2}\cdot\frac{|(\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1}))_{n}|}{\lambda_{1}n}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{r}\cdot{\cal O}(\sqrt{n\log n})}{\mu_{r}n^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{r\log n}}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

where the second step follows from
Lemma

C.1
and
\|\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})\|_{2}=\sqrt{n}
, and the third step follows from the fact that
(\boldsymbol{E}_{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1}))_{n}
is a sub-Gaussian random variable with parameter
{\cal O}(\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}_{n}(z_{1})\|_{2})={\cal O}(\alpha\sqrt{n})
.

Hence, combining Eq. (
E.52
) and Eq. (
E.53
) together, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)[:,1]\right\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle~\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{r\log n}}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{r\log n}}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,.

This implies

\displaystyle\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\left((\boldsymbol{V}_{r})_{\uparrow}\right)^{\dagger}\left(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{E}}^{Q}_{1}\right)_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}\right\|_{F}\leq\sqrt{r}\cdot\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\alpha\sqrt{r\log n}}{\mu_{r}n^{1.5}}+\frac{\alpha\sqrt{\log n}}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n^{1.5}}\right)\,,

(E.54)

Thus, by Eq. (
E.48
) and Eq. (
E.54
), we prove the first part of the lemma Eq. (
5.17a
).
For Eq. (
5.17b
), we can verify that the above proof still holds when we change
\uparrow
to
\downarrow
.

Therefore, we complete the proof of the lemma.
∎

E.3.3
Techincal claims

Claim E.5
.

Let
\boldsymbol{P}_{v}
be defined as Eq. (
B.3
). Then,

\boldsymbol{P}_{v}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}=\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,,

(E.55a)

\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-k}_{r}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}=\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-k}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}n}\right)^{k+1}\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}}\quad\forall k\geq 0\,.

(E.55b)

Proof.

We first prove Eq. (
E.55
).

By Eq. (
1.13
), we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}{\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})}_{\downarrow}\boldsymbol{P}_{v},\quad\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}{\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\,.

Since
{\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})}_{\downarrow}={\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})}_{\uparrow}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})
and
\boldsymbol{V}_{n}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})_{\uparrow}=\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})
, we also have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v},\quad\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\,.

Thus, LHS of Eq. (
E.55
) can be expressed as:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}_{v}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\right)\cdot\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)\cdot\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right)\cdot\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\cdot\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)\,,

where the second step follows from
\overline{z_{i}}=z_{i}^{-1}
for all
i\in[m]
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right)\cdot\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\cdot\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)-\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\left(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)\cdot\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}\,,

where the second step follows from the triangle inequality and
\|\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\|_{2}=1
.

By a slightly modified proof of
Corollary

B.2
, it is easy to show that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{V}_{n-1}(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}})-\boldsymbol{I}_{r}\right\|_{2}={\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,.

Together with
Lemma

1.8
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}(\boldsymbol{Q}_{\downarrow})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{Q}_{\uparrow}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\diag(\boldsymbol{z}_{\mathrm{dom}}^{-1})\right\|_{2}\leq{\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,

which proves the first part of the claim.

Next, we prove Eq. (
E.55b
).

When
k=0
, Eq. (
E.55b
) becomes

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{I}_{r}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,,

which follows from
Lemma

1.8
.

When
k\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-{k}}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-k}\right\|_{2}

(E.56)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-{(k-1)}}\left(\boldsymbol{I_{r}}-\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-{(k-1)}}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k-1)}\right)\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-1}\right)\right\|_{2}\,.

Since
\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\|_{2}={\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}n}\right)
by Eq. (
B.2
) and
\|\boldsymbol{I_{r}}-\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\|_{2}={\cal O}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)
by
Lemma

1.8
, the first term can be bounded by:

\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-{(k-1)}}\left(\boldsymbol{I_{r}}-\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\right)\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\leq{\cal O}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}n}\right)^{k}\cdot\frac{1}{\Delta_{\boldsymbol{z}}n}\right)\,.

(E.57)

Similarly, the second term can be bounded by:

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-{(k-1)}}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k-1)}\right)\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-1}\boldsymbol{P}_{v}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}

(E.58)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cal{\displaystyle O}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{r}n}\right)\cdot\left\|\boldsymbol{P}_{v}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{r}^{-{(k-1)}}\boldsymbol{P}^{\dagger}_{v}-\left(n\diag(\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathrm{dom}})\right)^{-(k-1)}\right\|_{2}

The third term can be bounded by:
